\displaystyle|\varrho_{k}^{\beta}(y;\Lambda)\partial_{y}^{\beta}T_{v2}g(y)|\lesssim_{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}\frac{\epsilon^{1/2}\norm{g}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\delta(\Lambda)^{2}\varrho^{\sigma_{1}+1/2}(y;\Lambda)\varrho_{k}^{\sigma_{2}-1/2}(y;\Lambda)},\quad\beta\in\{0,1\};

(8.78)

•

For
k\delta(\Lambda)\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\big|\varrho_{k}^{\beta}(y;\Lambda)\partial_{y}^{\beta}T_{v2}g(y)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}\frac{\epsilon^{1/2}\delta(\Lambda)\norm{g}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\delta(\Lambda)^{2}\varrho(y;\Lambda)\delta(\Lambda)^{\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2}}},\quad\beta\in\{0,1\};

(8.79)

•

For
|\lambda|\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}
,

\displaystyle\big|k^{-\beta}\partial_{y}^{\beta}T_{v2}g(y)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}\frac{1}{\big(k\delta(\Lambda)\big)^{1/2}}\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}k^{\sigma_{2}}\norm{g
}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\delta(\Lambda)\varrho^{\sigma_{1}}(y;\Lambda)},\quad k\delta(\Lambda)\geq 1,\,\,\beta\in\{0,1\},

(8.80a)

\displaystyle\big|\varrho_{k}^{2}(y;\Lambda)\partial_{y}^{2}T_{v2}g(y)\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}\frac{\norm{g}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\varrho^{\sigma_{1}+1/2}(y;\Lambda)\varrho_{k}^{\sigma_{2}-1/2}(y,\Lambda)},\quad k\delta(\Lambda)\leq 1.

(8.80b)

Remark 8.1
.

For
|\epsilon|^{\frac{1}{2}}\ll|\lambda|\ll 1
, (
8.78
) is sufficient to view
T_{v2}
as negligible as part of the proof of the limiting absorption principle. However, when
|\lambda|\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}
, we need to treat
T_{v2}
like
T_{I2}
and prove higher regularity estimates and favorable dependence on
k\delta(\Lambda)
.

Proof.

Integration by parts in (
8.77
) yields that
T_{v2}
can be written as

\displaystyle T_{v2}g(y)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)(1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z))\frac{\epsilon\partial_{z}^{2}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\,dz

\displaystyle=-\epsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\partial_{z}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz

\displaystyle-\epsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\right)\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz.

(8.81)

Similarly,
\partial_{y}T_{{}_{v}2}
can be written for
y\in\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}
as

\begin{split}\partial_{y}T_{v2}g(y)&=\partial_{y}\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)(1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z))\frac{\epsilon\partial_{z}^{2}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\,dz\\
&=-\epsilon\partial_{y}\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\partial_{z}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz\\
&-\epsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\partial_{y}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\right)\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz,\end{split}

(8.82)

and using (
2.7
), as

\begin{split}\partial_{y}T_{v2}g(y)=&-\epsilon\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(y)}{b(y)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{y}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(y)\\
&+\epsilon\big(V(y)+k^{2}\big)\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz\\
&-\epsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\partial_{y}(\partial_{z}+\partial_{y})\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz\\
&-\epsilon\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\partial_{y}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\partial_{z}\left(\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\right)\partial_{z}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz.\end{split}

Since
\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}
is supported a distance of approximately
\delta(\Lambda)
away from 0, Lemma
3.3
implies that for
y\in\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}
,

\displaystyle\left|\epsilon\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(y)}{b(y)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{y}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(y)\right|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{1/2}\norm{g}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\varrho^{3+\sigma_{1}}(y;\Lambda)\varrho_{k}^{\sigma_{2}}(y;\Lambda)}.

(8.83)

The bounds (
8.79
)-(
8.79
) then follow from (
8.83
) and Lemma
4.1
.

We now consider the case
|\lambda|\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}
. We no longer integrate by parts to remove a derivative from
\partial_{y}^{2}A_{\Theta}^{-1}
since the scale of variation of
\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)
is comparable or smaller than that of
A_{\Theta}^{-1}
.
Lemma
3.3
implies that for
y\in\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}
,

\left|\epsilon\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(y)}{b(y)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(y)\right|\lesssim\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\norm{g}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\varrho^{3+\sigma_{1}}(y;\Lambda)\varrho_{k}^{1+\sigma_{2}}(y;\Lambda)},

(8.84)

which, in combination with Lemma
4.1

and (
8.84
), implies that for
k\delta(\Lambda)\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\left|k^{-\beta}\partial_{y}^{\beta}T_{v2}g(y)\right|\lesssim\frac{1}{\big(k\delta(\Lambda)\big)^{1/2}}\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}k^{\sigma_{2}}\norm{g
}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\delta(\Lambda)\varrho^{\sigma_{1}}(y;\Lambda)},\quad\beta\in\{0,1\}.

(8.85)

For the second derivative estimates, we simply use the identity that

\displaystyle\partial_{y}^{2}T_{v2}g(y)=

\displaystyle-\epsilon\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(y)}{b(y)-\lambda-i\alpha}\partial_{y}^{2}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(y)

\displaystyle+(V(y)+k^{2})\int_{\mathbb{T}_{\mathfrak{p}}}\mathcal{G}_{k}(y,z;\Lambda)\frac{1-\varphi_{\frac{\delta(\Lambda)}{{3}}}(z)}{b(z)-\lambda-i\alpha}\epsilon\partial_{z}^{2}A_{\Theta}^{-1}(\eta_{\delta(\Lambda)}b^{\prime\prime}g)(z)\,dz.

(8.86)

Therefore for
k\delta(\Lambda)\leq 1

\varrho_{k}^{2}(y;\Lambda)|\partial_{y}^{2}T_{v2}g(y)|\lesssim_{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}\frac{\norm{g}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}}{\varrho^{\sigma_{1}}(y;\Lambda)\varrho_{k}^{\sigma_{2}}(y;\Lambda)},

(8.87)

which concludes the proof.
∎

The following is an immediate corollary of Lemma
8.9
.

Corollary 8.3
.

Assume that
(\alpha,\lambda)\in(-\sigma_{0}\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},1)\times\Sigma_{j,\delta_{0}}
, and
k\in\mathbb{Z}\cap[1,\infty)
. Then for all
\epsilon\in(0,1/8)
the following bounds hold for
T_{v2}:X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})\to X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})

with
\sigma_{1}\in[0,1]
,
\sigma_{1}+\sigma_{2}\in(-2,1)
.

\displaystyle\norm{\tc{v2}{}}_{X^{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}(\mathfrak{M})}\lesssim_{\sigma_{1},\sigma_{2}}\begin{cases}\frac{\epsilon^{1/2}}{\delta(\Lambda)^{2}},&{\rm if}\,\,\lambda\in\Sigma_{j,\delta_{0}},\\
\frac{\epsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}}{\delta(\Lambda)\langle k\delta(\Lambda)\rangle^{1/2}},&{\rm if}\,\,|\lambda|\lesssim\epsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{ and }k\delta(\Lambda)\geq 1.\end{cases}

(8.88)
